While qualitative evidence supports the existence of this effect, its magnitude has not been systematically characterized. This study quantifies subliminal behavioral transfer ratios by steering two teacher models (Llama-2-7B-Chat and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct) at varying steering str","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-09T06:52:49Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_11270.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是 distillation 里的一个安全问题：如果 teacher 模型被轻度到中度地“去拒答化”，学生模型在只看表面无害数据的情况下，会有多大比例把这种有害行为倾向一起学过去。已有工作已经说明 subliminal learning 存在，但缺的是量化曲线：teacher 的行为被破坏多少，student 会转移多少；这种转移是平滑增长、阈值式跃迁，还是强依赖模型家族。作者把这个问题具体化为“subliminal behavioral transfer ratio”的测量。","importance":"对预训练本身是间接相关，对 post-train、distillation、模型供应链安全更直接。它的价值不在提出新的蒸馏算法，而在给出一个可复现实验框架，去测量 teacher 行为偏移如何通过 benign-only distillation 渗入 student。对基座研究员的启发主要有两点。第一，模型行为不是只靠显式训练样本内容传递，生成分布中的细微风格和拒答边界也会被 imitation 学走。第二，这种转移可能存在明显的 family-dependent scaling，这意味着“同样的 teacher compromise 强度”不能简单外推到别的模型族。","technical_approach":"方法设计比较直接，但控制变量做得较清楚。作者选了两个 7B chat/instruct teacher：Llama-2-7B-Chat 和 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct。先按照 refusal-direction 相关工作的方法，构造 harmful / harmless 对比数据集，在每层最后 token 的 residual hidden state 上取均值差，得到 refusal direction。Llama 选第 15 层方向，Qwen 选第 18 层方向，并在一段连续层范围内注入该方向：Llama 在 10-17 层，Qwen 在 9-16 层。通过负的 steering coefficient α 抑制 refusal，实验用 α∈{-0.05,-0.10,-0.15,-0.20,-0.25}。\n\n接着，为了隔离“隐性转移”而不是“训练数据直接污染”，作者只从 benign 数据里采样 1000 个 prompt，来自 Mechanistic Anomaly Detection 数据集的 normal_benign_train split。teacher 在不同 steering 强度下对这些 benign prompt 生成回答，使用 greedy decoding，过滤掉重复和生成失败样本，只保留表面无害的 teacher 输出作为蒸馏语料。然后用这些数据对 student 做 imitation-style distillation / SFT，使 student 从未直接见过 jailbreak 或 harmful prompt。\n\n最后，用 100 个 JailbreakBench prompt 测 student 的安全退化程度，评估器是 GPT-4.1。作者把 teacher 的安全退化与 student 的安全退化做比值，定义 transfer ratio τ，用它来刻画“teacher 被破坏 1 单位，student 学走多少”。这个框架的优点是把三个环节拆开：可控地破坏 teacher、只用 benign 数据蒸馏、再在独立 harmful benchmark 上测 student，从而尽量把因果链收紧。","experiments":"核心结果是两种不同的 scaling 形态。Llama-2 家族表现出较明显的阈值效应：当 steering 强度超过大约 α≈-0.15 后，transfer ratio τ 会跳到约 0.25-0.32 的区间，说明在较弱 compromise 下隐性转移有限，但一旦 teacher 的 refusal 被压到某个程度，student 会开始稳定继承这类退化。Qwen2.5 则更接近平滑且更高的转移曲线，τ 最高可到 0.61，表示 teacher 行为偏移有更大比例渗入 student。\n\n从实验设计看，作者使用 148 个 harmful 和 165 个 harmless prompt 来提取 refusal direction；蒸馏数据来自 1000 个 benign prompt；安全评测使用 100 个 JailbreakBench prompt，并由 GPT-4.1 作为 judge。摘要和正文片段里没有给出更细的 student 训练超参、模型大小变化或完整误差条信息，因此能确认的重点是趋势而不是精确绝对值。\n\n如果把结果翻成更工程化的话：同样是 7B teacher、同样是 benign-only distillation，隐性行为转移既不是零，也不是统一常数。它可能在某些模型族里呈现“过了某个点才明显泄漏”，在另一些模型族里则从较小 compromise 就持续累积。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和 Methodology，把作者如何定义 transfer ratio、如何避免直接数据污染弄明白。这里是整篇文章最重要的部分，因为如果 benign-only 控制不严，所谓 subliminal transfer 就会和普通数据污染混在一起。\n\n然后看 refusal direction 的提取与注入细节，包括为什么选特定层、为什么在一段层区间内施加 steering。再看主结果图，重点不是某个单点数值，而是 Llama 的 threshold-like 曲线和 Qwen 的 smoother/higher curve 这两个形态差异。\n\n最后快速看评测设置，尤其是 GPT-4.1 judge 和 JailbreakBench 的使用方式，确认它测的是 harmful compliance 而不是别的能力退化。若只打算花 15-25 分钟，读 Introduction、Methodology、主结果图和讨论部分即可。若要深挖，优先检查数据过滤规则、student 训练细节以及 transfer ratio 的具体定义是否对 judge 偏差敏感。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a safety question in distillation: if a teacher model is behaviorally compromised by suppressing its refusal tendency, how much of that compromised behavior transfers to a student trained only on superficially benign data? Prior work established the existence of subliminal learning, but not its scaling law. The missing piece is quantitative: how transfer varies with the degree of teacher compromise, whether the curve is smooth or thresholded, and whether the pattern depends on model family.","importance":"This is more relevant to post-training and model-supply-chain safety than to core pretraining, but it still matters for foundation-model researchers. The main lesson is that behavioral properties can transfer through the teacher’s output distribution even when the training corpus contains no explicit harmful examples. A second useful point is that transfer is family-dependent. The same intervention strength on the teacher does not imply the same downstream risk across model families, which argues against treating distillation safety as a simple data-filtering problem.","technical_approach":"$4d","experiments":"$4e","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the introduction and methodology. The most important thing to verify is how the paper separates subliminal transfer from ordinary data contamination. If that control is weak, the whole claim becomes much less interesting.\n\nThen read the refusal-direction extraction and steering details, especially the layer choice and the multi-layer injection range. After that, go straight to the main results figure and focus on the shape difference: threshold-like transfer in Llama versus smoother and larger transfer in Qwen. That contrast is the main empirical takeaway.\n\nFinally, skim the evaluation section to understand how GPT-4.1 judging is used on JailbreakBench and what exactly counts as harmful compliance. If you have 15-25 minutes, the introduction, methodology, main results, and discussion are enough. Although this approach successfully improves the student's no-context performance, we identify an interesting and previously unstudied phenomenon: in many settings, reintroducing the original privileged context to the distilled student actually degrades its performance, even on instances it alre","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-10T03:43:43Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_11627.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是 on-policy distillation 里一个很容易被忽略的问题：学生模型把特权上下文 internalize 之后，推理时去掉上下文确实能变强，但如果把原来的上下文重新加回来，性能反而常常下降。作者把这个现象叫 context-induced degradation。更准确地说，现有目标只优化“无上下文学生要模仿有上下文教师”，却没有约束学生在“有上下文视角”下的行为，因此 internalization 可能只是把一条路径训好了，而不是让上下文真正变成冗余信息。论文提出的核心要求是 context removability：如果知识真的被吸收到参数里，那么上下文重新出现时，模型行为不应被破坏。","importance":"这项工作对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对 pretrain 与 post-train 的接口很有启发，尤其是 context distillation、system prompt internalization、CoT 蒸馏和带 scaffold 的 on-policy 训练。它指出一个常见误区：把外部提示蒸馏进模型，不等于模型学会了“稳定地拥有这份能力”。如果训练目标只看 no-context 路径，模型可能学到一种脆弱的补偿机制，而不是形成真正可重入、可移除的内部表征。这个判断对后续做 reasoning distillation、tool-use scaffold distillation、甚至 RL 中的 privileged information training 都有参考价值。贡献量级属于训练目标层面的明确修正，方法很小，但问题定义很扎实。","technical_approach":"作者先把问题形式化成两个 student view：q_x = gθ(y|x) 和 q_c = gθ(y|x,c)。标准 on-policy distillation with privileged context 只最小化无上下文学生 q_x 与有上下文教师 f(y|x,c) 之间的 KL，因此只保证 privileged fidelity，不保证 q_x 和 q_c 一致。\n\n基于这个观察，论文提出 No-Context Anchoring, NCA。做法很简单：把学生在无上下文下的输出分布当作 stop-gradient anchor，再对学生有上下文视角的输出加一个 consistency KL，惩罚 q_c 偏离 q_x。直观上，这是在告诉模型：上下文可以帮助你学，但一旦你已经学会了，它重新出现时不该把你推到另一个行为模式里。\n\n这个设计有几个值得注意的点。第一，anchor 选 no-context 而不是 context-conditioned teacher。原因是部署目标本来就是无上下文推理，作者希望把已经 internalized 的路径当成主路径，再把有上下文视角拉回这条路径，而不是反过来。第二，使用 stop-gradient 很关键，否则两个视角可能互相拖拽，削弱主目标。第三，它只增加一次额外 forward pass，训练代价相对温和。\n\n论文还提出了一个概念区分：privileged fidelity 解决的是“学生无上下文时能否达到教师有上下文时的能力”，而 context removability 解决的是“上下文是否已经变成真正冗余的信息”。这两个目标并不等价。作者进一步用表示分析说明，NCA 不只是让输出更像，而是让有无上下文时的 hidden states 也更接近，说明它在一定程度上改变了内部表征，而不是只做表层校正。","experiments":"实验覆盖 12 个配置，跨不同领域和模型家族。核心结果有三条。第一，context-induced degradation 很常见，多数设置里重新加入上下文会让准确率下降，并且生成长度膨胀。作者把设置分成三类，其中 Regime A 占 7/12：学生无上下文已经做得不错，但一加上下文就被扰动，出现明显 accuracy drop 和 harm rate。第二，NCA 在 11/12 个设置里降低了这种 harm，并在多数设置里提升了 context-conditioned accuracy。第三，它基本消除了 response-length inflation，而且在不少设置里连 no-context performance 也一起改善，这一点很关键，因为 no-context 才是原始训练目标。\n\n论文还定义了 Harm = P(q_c wrong | q_x correct)，这个指标比单纯 accuracy drop 更有信息量，因为它直接衡量“原本答对的样本被上下文重新弄错”的概率。机制分析部分显示，加入 NCA 后，有无上下文两条路径的表示更接近，支持作者关于 context removability 的论点。\n\n从可读到的内容看，这篇论文的亮点不在大规模数字，而在问题诊断和指标设计。它把“蒸馏成功”拆成了两个不同维度，并证明只优化其中一个会留下明显缺口。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Problem Formulation，把 privileged fidelity、context removability、harm failure mode in on-policy distillation with privileged context. A student can learn to perform well without the context at inference time, yet when the original context is reintroduced, performance often gets worse rather than staying unchanged. The authors call this context-induced degradation. Their claim is that standard objectives only optimize one side of the problem: the no-context student should match the context-conditioned teacher. That does not guarantee that the student has truly internalized the privileged information in a stable way. For robust internalization, the context should become removable: adding it back should be behaviorally harmless.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it is highly relevant to the pretrain-posttrain interface. The result matters for context distillation, system-prompt internalization, chain-of-thought distillation, and any training setup where privileged information is available during learning but absent at deployment. The main lesson is subtle and important: good no-context performance does not imply that the model has formed a stable internal representation of the privileged signal. It may instead have learned a brittle compensation path. activit","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-10T10:22:49Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_11893.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL","q-bio.NC"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作关心两个相连的问题。第一，LLM 在做演绎推理时，内部表征是否真的和人脑中负责推理的神经活动存在可测的对应关系，而不只是和语言区对齐。第二，如果这种对应关系存在，脑信号能否不只用于解释模型，而是反过来作为干预信号，直接提升模型在推理任务上的鲁棒性，尤其是把原本会答错的样本拉回正确轨道。论文把问题限定在 deductive reasoning，并且刻意区分 aggregate-level alignment 与 reasoning-type-specific alignment，避免把总体相关性误读成机制层面的充分一致。","importance":"对基座研究的直接影响不在于它给出了一条可立即规模化的预训练路线，而在于它提供了一个新的训练信号来源和一个新的机制分析视角。更具体地说，它说明 next-token 训练得到的表征已经部分进入了与人类推理网络相近的子空间，但这种对齐并不完整，且在细粒度推理类型上明显不足。这一点对“语言建模是否足以诱导稳健推理”这个老问题给了更细的证据。更有意思的是，作者没有把 brain alignment 当作终点，而是把它当作 intervention target：只在模型即将出错的区域做表征修正。这种思路和常见的 post-hoc probing 不同，也和直接加一个辅助 loss 的做法不同。对预训练研究员来说，启发主要有三点：一是可以把外部认知信号视作 representation regularizer；二是模型错误可能集中在某些可识别的表征方向上；三是“对齐”与“性能提升”不是同一个目标，后者更依赖局部、条件化的干预。","technical_approach":"方法分两部分。第一部分是测量 LLM 与人脑推理网络的表征对应。作者使用任务型 fMRI 数据，聚焦既往文献中与演绎推理相关的脑区，而不是泛化到整个 cortex。对多个开源 LLM，在处理推理题时抽取内部 hidden representations，再用 neural predictivity 指标评估这些表征对脑活动 explainable variance 的解释比例。这里有两个关键设计。其一，既看跨全部推理样本聚合后的 predictivity，也看按具体 reasoning type 切分后的 predictivity；前者高，后者低，说明模型学到的是较粗的共享结构，而不是稳定覆盖各类推理机制。其二，加入未训练模型和其他脑网络作为对照，验证这种对应不是随机几何相似，也不是对任何高层认知区都同样成立。\n\n第二部分是利用脑信号做模型干预。作者提出两种方法。NARI 是 inference-time 的表征干预：先在 joint model-brain representational structure 中识别与“错误回答”相关、但可被脑信号校正的方向，然后在推理时对模型中间层表示做定向修正，把表示从错误区域推向更接近正确解答对应的区域。它更像 activation steering，但 steering vector 不是纯模型内生统计量，而是由脑活动和模型表征的对应结构共同确定。NARF 是 parameter-level 的微调版本：把这种 neural guidance 融入训练，对模型参数做小幅更新，使其更倾向于形成与正确推理相关的内部表示。两者的共同点是都不追求全局 brain imitation，而是只利用那些对纠错有用的神经约束。\n\n从方法取舍看，这比直接把 fMRI 当监督标签去回归整段脑活动更克制。原因很清楚：脑信号噪声大、样本少、跨被试差异强，若做全局拟合，很容易学到与任务无关的个体差异。作者转而利用 joint subspace 和 error-correction framing，把问题变成“哪些脑相关方向能帮助纠错”，这更接近可用信号的上限。","experiments":"论文给出的核心结果有四类。第一，表征对齐方面，LLM 在演绎推理相关脑区上的 aggregate neural predictivity 大约达到 explainable variance 的 76%，明显高于未训练模型对照；但在按具体 reasoning type 划分后，predictivity 只有约 27% 左右，说明细粒度机制对齐仍然有限。第二，选择性方面，这种对齐对 reasoning network 更强，而不是同样出现在 multiple-demand network 或 core language network，支持作者关于“并非单纯语言区对齐”的论点。第三，干预效果方面，NARI 和 NARF 都能把一部分原本错误的回答改成正确，说明脑信号不仅能解释模型，还能作为有效的纠错引导。摘要截断没有给出完整表格数字，但从正文叙述看，提升主要集中在困难样本和原始模型易错的推理类型上，而不是对所有样本平均抬升。第四，作者还做了若干控制实验，验证提升不是来自额外 test-time compute、提示模板变化或简单的 representation smoothing，而是与 brain-guided direction 的特异性有关。\n\n值得注意的实验洞察是：总体高 predictivity 并不自动转化为高推理准确率，反过来，局部的 neural guidance 却能在错误样本上产生可见收益。这说明“像脑”不是单一标量目标，更像是某些关键子空间上的约束问题。对做预训练的人来说，这比单看相关系数更有信息量。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓三处。第一，看引言和任务设定，确认作者讨论的是 deductive reasoning 的哪几类题，以及 fMRI 数据是如何采集和对齐到样本级别的。第二，看 neural predictivity 的定义、explainable variance 的估计方式、以及 reasoning network / MD network / language network 的对照设置；这是判断结果是否可信的关键。第三，看 NARI 和 NARF 的实现细节，尤其是干预发生在哪些层、如何识别“将要答错”的表示、以及 brain-model joint structure 是怎样构造的。\n\n如果时间有限，优先看主文中的方法图、predictivity 主结果图、以及干预前后错误样本翻转的案例分析，预计 25 到 35 分钟可以抓住主线。若要深挖，建议重点检查附录里的被试间一致性、不同模型规模的差异、以及是否存在数据泄漏或题型偏置。真正值得追问的点有两个：一是这种 brain-guided direction 是否能迁移到未见题型；二是如果把脑信号替换成更便宜的行为信号或 verifier 信号，是否能复现类似收益。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles two linked questions. First, when LLMs perform deductive reasoning, do their internal representations align with neural activity in human reasoning-related regions, rather than merely with language areas? Second, if such alignment exists, can brain signals be used not only for analysis but also as an intervention signal to improve reasoning performance, especially on cases the model would otherwise answer incorrectly? The study is deliberately scoped to deductive reasoning and distinguishes aggregate alignment from reasoning-type-specific alignment, which is an important methodological choice.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is not that it offers an immediately scalable recipe, but that it introduces a different kind of training signal and a sharper way to think about representation quality. The results suggest that next-token-trained models already enter a subspace partially shared with human reasoning networks, yet this alignment is incomplete and much weaker at fine-grained reasoning-type resolution. That is a more informative answer to the long-running question of whether language modeling alone induces robust reasoning. The more interesting move is conceptual: brain alignment is treated as a means for targeted correction, not as an end in itself. For foundation-model work, the main takeaways are that external cognitive signals can act as representation regularizers, model errors may concentrate in steerable directions of hidden space, and global alignment metrics are not the same thing as performance-relevant local interventions.","technical_approach":"$51","experiments":"$52","reading_guide":"$53","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"机构强，但离 pretrain 很远；把它当 reasoning representation 的外部探针看，先看 predictivity 结果，干预部分先持保留。","en":"Strong institutions, but far from pretraining; treat it as an external probe of reasoning representations, and trust the predictivity analysis more than the intervention claims."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Peking University","Tsinghua University","Microsoft Research Asia","Technology, Peking University","Tsinghua","State Key Lab of General AI"],"authors":["Mingqing Xiao","Kai Du","Zhouchen Lin"],"notable_authors":["Zhouchen Lin"],"tags":["mechanistic-analysis","reasoning","brain-alignment","fMRI","representation-steering"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11893","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.11893","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.12138","title":"Unstable Features, Reproducible Subspaces: Understanding Seed Dependence in Sparse Autoencoders","abstract":"Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret neural network representations, but their utility depends on whether the learned features are reproducible across training runs. 是可重复、可命名、可比较的基本单元；这篇论文给出的结论更细：稳定特征往往承载主要的功能信号，不稳定特征单独看不可靠，但它们并非纯噪声，而是常常共同张成可复现的低秩子空间。这个判断会直接影响如何使用 SAE 做训练动态分析、特征追踪、以及跨 checkpoint 或跨模型比较。对基座研究员来说，启发在于不要把单个 feature 的可解释性和其因果重要性混为一谈，也不要把 seed 不一致简单理解为方法失效。","technical_approach":"作者提出 per-feature stability 这一粒度更细的指标，而不是只做两组 SAE 字典的整体匹配分数。具体做法是：在同一模型层、同一数据、同一超参下训练多组不同 seed 的 SAE，把某一组作为 anchor；对 anchor 中每个 feature，用 decoder 向量与其他 seed 的 SAE 做 many-to-one 最大余弦匹配，若相似度超过阈值 θ=0.7，则记为该次“重现”。跨多个独立运行统计后，得到该 feature 的 reappearance probability，也就是 feature stability。作者说明这种 many-to-one 近似与 Hungarian 一对一匹配得到的 matched set 几乎一致，IoU 达到 0.978±0.001，因此计算上更便宜但结论基本不变。\n\n在这个指标基础上，论文系统比较 stable 与 unstable features 的差异。分析维度包括：重建贡献、对下游预测的边际作用、激活统计、token 触发模式、自动解释文本、以及 decoder-space 几何。核心发现有两层。第一层是功能不对称：高稳定特征承担了大部分 reconstruction- 和 prediction-relevant signal；低稳定特征的边际作用弱，且更容易被低频、表层、局部 token 模式触发。第二层是子空间可复现而 feature 不可识别：许多个体上不稳定的 feature，在不同 seed 下虽然一一对不上，但它们共同覆盖的低维子空间却高度一致。作者还在合成数据上构造低秩 ground-truth feature，验证 SAE 可以恢复正确子空间，却未必恢复同一组 latent basis。\n\n论文还做了一个有意思的构造实验：从多次训练得到的 feature 池中去重，按稳定性筛选出跨 seed 更一致的 feature，再重新组装 SAE。结果显示，在这个 feature-pool construction 设定下，更高 explained variance 可以和更高平均 feature probability 同时出现，说明稳定性与重建质量不必然冲突。这一点是在回应一个常见担忧：为了追求可重复性，是否必须牺牲表示能力。作者给出的答案是，至少在这种构造式 setting 下，不需要。","experiments":"实验覆盖多个模型、层位、字典大小和 SAE 变体，包括 Vanilla ReLU+L1、TopK、BatchTopK、HierarchicalTopK 和 JumpReLU，范围比只比较单一 SAE 配方更扎实。最关键的数字有两个。第一，many-to-one 匹配与 Hungarian matching 的 matched feature set 高度一致，IoU 为 0.978±0.001，说明作者提出的 per-feature stability 估计在工程上可行。第二，稳定特征与不稳定特征在功能贡献上呈明显分化：稳定特征保留了大部分重建和预测相关信号，而不稳定特征的单独边际增益较小。摘要没有给出所有分层数字，但正文强调这种趋势在不同模型、层和 SAE 变体上都相当一致。\n\n更有信息量的是几组分析。自动解释和激活统计显示，不稳定特征更常对应低频 surface-form triggers，而不是语义或功能上更稳的抽象模式。decoder-space 几何分析则表明，这些不稳定特征常聚成可复现的低秩区域。合成实验进一步排除了“只是自然语言数据太脏”的解释，因为在已知 ground-truth 的低秩 setting 中，同样出现了 basis 不可识别但 subspace 可恢复的现象。最后，feature-pool construction 实验表明，经过去重和调参后，由稳定跨 seed 特征构成的 SAE 可以同时获得更高 explained variance 和更高平均重现概率，反驳了稳定性与性能天然对立的直觉。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议按问题链来读。先看方法部分对 stability 的定义，尤其是 reappearance probability、相似度阈值、以及 many-to-one matching 为什么足够。然后看 stable vs unstable 的主结果图，抓住功能不对称这个核心结论。接着看 decoder-space / subspace 分析和合成实验，因为真正的新意不只是“feature 不稳定”，而是“feature 不稳定但 subspace 可复现”。最后再看 feature-pool construction，那部分回答了这个发现是否能转化为更好的 SAE 构造。\n\n如果只想判断这篇论文是否影响你当前的 interpretability pipeline，读主文方法、主结果和合成实验，大约 30 分钟足够。若你在做跨 checkpoint 特征追踪、用 SAE feature 做因果干预、或把 SAE 当训练诊断工具，建议再仔细看附录里的不同 SAE 变体、不同字典大小和不同层位结果。真正值得记住的一点是：单 feature 的名字和可视化并不等于稳定的机制单元，很多时候更稳的是子空间而不是基向量。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks what seed dependence in sparse autoencoders actually means. When SAE features fail to reproduce across random initializations, does that imply the features are not real, or does it mean different runs are using different bases to represent the same underlying activation subspace?","importance":"This is not a direct pretraining-method paper, but it matters for the reliability of interpretability tools that many foundation-model researchers now use. A common implicit assumption is that SAE features are reproducible, nameable, and comparable units. The paper argues for a more careful view: stable features carry most of the functionally relevant signal, while unstable features are weak as individual units but often collectively span reproducible low-rank subspaces. That has practical consequences for feature tracking across checkpoints, comparing models, and using SAEs to study training dynamics. The main lesson is that interpretability of a single feature should not be conflated with causal importance or reproducibility.","technical_approach":"$54","experiments":"$55","reading_guide":"$56","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"做 SAE interpretability 的人该看；它没有改变表征可分解这个大 prior，但会改变你对“单个 feature 可复现性”的读法，先看稳定性定义和子空间结论。","en":"Worth reading if you use SAEs: it does not kill the decomposability prior, but it should change how you interpret single-feature reproducibility; read the stability metric and the subspace result first."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Gleb Gerasimov","Timofei Rusalev","Nikita Balagansky","Daniil Laptev","Vadim Kurochkin","Daniil Gavrilov"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["sparse-autoencoder","interpretability","seed-dependence","feature-stability","representation-analysis"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12138","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.12138","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.11961","title":"Categorical Prior Lock-in: Why In-Context Learning Fails for Structured Data","abstract":"Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as conditional generators for structured data, relying on in-context learning (ICL) to adapt to new distributions without parameter updates. We investigate the limits of ICL for structured generation under distribution mismatch, using high-cardinality tabular data as a controlled test case, and identify a structural failure mode we term \\textit{categorical prior lock-in}: the inability of ICL to update the model's prior over token distributions 先验，而不是按提示中的少量样本去重建新的类别分布。结果不是简单的精度下降，而是少数类直接消失、边缘类别完全不被采样、联合分布也随之失真。这个问题在数值字段上没有同样严重，说明失败点不是“模型不会照着例子生成”，而是“模型无法通过上下文完成全局类别先验重置”。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有三层。第一，它把 ICL 的边界讲得更具体了：ICL 对映射关系、格式约束、局部模式有适应能力，但对需要改写全局 token 先验的任务，尤其是高基数类别分布迁移，能力明显不足。第二，这个现象和预训练数据分布、tokenizer 切分、指令模型的解码先验直接相关，因此它不是一个单纯的应用问题，而是预训练表示与后续条件生成之间的接口问题。第三，论文还展示了 LoRA 可以补上这类分布迁移能力，但代价是样本邻近度显著上升，出现更强的记忆和泄漏风险。这一点对“用 PEFT 替代 ICL 做私有数据合成”很有现实意义：分布 fidelity 和 privacy 不是同向变化的。","technical_approach":"方法上并不复杂，但实验设计比较干净。作者选了一个信用卡交易表格数据集，包含 7 个数值特征、4 个类别特征和极低基率的 fraud 标签。其中最关键的是 job 字段有 494 个类别，且分布极度长尾，最常见类别也只占 0.75%。这相当于把模型放在一个很难依赖语义泛化、只能靠分布建模的压力测试里。实验对象是 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 和 Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3，两者都用统一的 schema prompt，然后比较 zero-shot、1/5/10-shot ICL 和 LoRA 微调。生成时逐条采样 JSON 记录，经过严格的语法与类型校验，不合法就重采，直到得到 1 万条有效样本。评估分三类：一是结构可靠性，用 invalid generation rate；二是统计保真度，用每个字段的 TVD，比对真实与合成边缘分布；三是记忆风险，用 DCR ratio 和 exact duplication rate。作者还单独检查 fraud 基率是否能被重现，并分析联合依赖是否随类别失真而恶化。核心分析点在于：随着 in-context 示例增加，数值字段的 TVD 会稳定下降，但类别字段，尤其是高基数字段，TVD 很快进入平台期，少数类始终采不出来。提示工程在测试范围内无效，说明问题不在 prompt wording，而在模型内部先验无法被上下文覆盖。LoRA 则通过参数更新完成了这种“全局分布重校准”。","experiments":"关键实验信号很一致。数据集里 fraud 真实占比只有 0.58%，job 有 494 个类别且高度不平衡。两种 7B instruct 模型都表现出相同趋势：few-shot ICL 随着示例数从 1 增到 10，数值特征保真度继续改善，但类别特征出现明显天花板，尤其是长尾类别和少数类几乎不被生成。论文强调这不是单个字段的问题，因为类别边缘分布失真会进一步破坏跨字段依赖，例如地理、职业、欺诈标签之间的联合结构。LoRA 训练只用 10% 或 50% 真实数据，就能显著改善 marginal 和 joint fidelity，说明参数更新确实能完成 ICL 做不到的分布迁移。但所有 LoRA 配置的 DCR ratio 都低于 1.0，落入作者定义的潜在记忆风险区间；同时 exact duplication 也需要结合看，意味着模型生成样本更贴近训练记录。论文的一个重要观察是：ICL 的失败不是“样本不够多所以还没学会”，而是随着 shot 增加后类别分布误差不再继续下降，呈现结构性停滞。这个结论比单纯报告下游 utility 更有解释力。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先看 可以”。如果时间有限，先抓三件事：一，job 这类高基数长尾字段的 ceiling 现象；二，LoRA 提升 fidelity 但带来更高邻近度；三，prompt engineering 基本无效。预计 20 到 30 分钟可以完成有效泛读。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise question: when an instruction-tuned LLM is used as a structured data generator, can in-context learning actually shift the model to a new target distribution without parameter updates? The answer is largely no for high-cardinality categorical variables. The authors identify a failure mode they call categorical prior lock-in: the model remains anchored to token-level priors inherited from pretraining and cannot reliably reconstruct a new categorical distribution from a handful of examples in context. The failure is not just mild degradation. Rare classes disappear entirely, minority labels are never reproduced, and downstream joint dependencies break as a consequence. Numerical fields improve with more examples, which makes the result sharper: the limitation is specifically about updating global categorical priors, not about following examples in general.","importance":"This is useful for pretraining-oriented readers because it turns a vague statement about ICL limitations into a concrete distributional claim. First, it suggests that ICL is good at local adaptation, formatting, and conditional mapping, but weak at rewriting global token priors when the target distribution is far from the pretrained one. Second, the phenomenon is tightly connected to pretraining data, tokenization, and decoding priors, so it is better viewed as a pretrain-to-inference interface issue than as a narrow synthetic tabular data problem. Third, the paper shows that LoRA can repair the distribution shift, but at the cost of much higher proximity to training records. That trade-off matters for anyone considering PEFT instead of ICL for private data synthesis: fidelity and privacy do not move in the same direction.","technical_approach":"$57","experiments":"$58","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the introduction and the evaluation section to understand the claimed failure mode and the role of TVD and DCR. The mechanism discussion in Section 6 is the part to keep if you care about why ICL fails here while LoRA succeeds. If time is limited, focus on three things: the ceiling on the long-tail job field, the fidelity-versus-privacy trade-off under LoRA, and the fact that prompt engineering does not materially change the outcome. This raises a basic robustness question, crucial to reproducibility and reliability: how sensitive are MD dynamics to their inputs? We focus on initialization, often itself a pretrained or previously aligned model. Quadratic-regularized MD, including GD and Mahalanobis geometries, is well-known to","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-09T20:33:01Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_11431.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是 mirror descent 对初始化扰动的有限步敏感性。问题很具体：当优化不是从随机点开始，而是从一个预训练、SFT 或已对齐的 checkpoint 开始时，初始点的微小差异会不会被后续更新放大。论文给出的结论是，会，而且在非二次 regularizer 下，这种放大可以比 Euclidean 几何下的 gradient descent 严重得多。作者证明，即便目标函数是凸且光滑的，regularizer 也是强凸、光滑、在欧氏范数下条件良好的，只要 mirror map 不是二次型，T 步后的输出差异也可能达到 e^{Ω(ηT)} 级别；而 GD 在同类设定下保持非扩张或至多温和增长。对 RLHF、KL-regularized policy optimization、DPO/GRPO 一类把参考模型和初始化都当成训练输入的流程，这个问题直接关系到复现性、checkpoint 选择敏感性，以及小扰动是否会被训练动力学放大。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义不在于它提出了新的训练 recipe，而在于它把一个常被经验讨论的问题做成了严格结论：非欧几里得几何带来的好处，不是免费得到的，稳定性可能明显变差。很多 post-train 或 RL-style 更新都可以写成某种 KL/Bregman 正则化的 mirror descent 近似，这篇论文说明，若把初始化看成上游预训练的输出，那么下游优化的敏感性可能不是噪声，而是算法内生属性。贡献分三层。第一层是理论分离：给出 MD 与 GD 在 initialization stability 上的指数级 separation。第二层是机制拆解：区分了两种来源，一种来自非二次几何本身，即便 regularizer 条件良好也会发生；另一种来自 entropy 这类 near-boundary ill-conditioning。第三层是修正方案：提出 anchored MD 变体，在保留 Bregman 几何的同时显式控制初始化敏感性。对做 RL pretrain、continual pretrain、alignment-as-optimization 的人，这篇论文提供了一个更稳的分析框架：不要只看收敛和最终 reward，也要看训练映射对初始 checkpoint 的 Lipschitz 性。","technical_approach":"$59","experiments":"这篇论文以理论为主，实验不是重点，但文中的数值验证承担了两个作用：一是展示下界构造不是病态符号游戏，而是实际可观察的动力学；二是比较两种 anchored 变体的稳定化效果。关键结果有几类。其一，在三维 well-conditioned 非二次 regularizer 的构造例子里，作者数值展示了输出差异随步数 T 近似按 exp(c·ηT) 增长，并在达到某个常数量级后饱和；同样设定下 GD 或二次 regularizer 不出现这种增长。其二，在 simplex 上的 entropy MD 中，即便目标是线性的，靠近边界或在高维均匀初始化下，也能观察到与理论一致的指数敏感性。其三，Initialization-Anchored MD 在条件良好的设定下显著抑制扰动放大，但在 entropy 几何、初始化靠近边界时效果会退化；Fixed-Anchor MD 则更稳，能在 ill-conditioned 情况下维持理论预测的稳定趋势。论文的主要“数字”是理论阶而不是 benchmark 分数：MD 的敏感性下界是 e^{Ω(ηT)}，Initialization-Anchored MD 的稳定性界是 O(ε + 1/√(T log T))，Fixed-Anchor MD 是 O(1/T)，两者优化误差都保持在 O(log T / T) 量级。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三件事。第一，看 introduction 里作者如何把初始化解释成“上游模型输出”，这一步把纯优化问题和 LLM post-train 接上了。第二，看两个 lower bound：一个是三维、well-conditioned、非二次 regularizer 的构造；另一个是 simplex 上 entropy MD 的 lower/upper bound。前者回答“不是坏条件数导致的”，后者回答“KL 几何里为什么尤其危险”。第三，看 anchored MD 两个变体的定理陈述和假设差别，尤其是为什么 initialization anchor 在 ill-conditioned regularizer 下会失效，而 fixed anchor 不会。\n\n如果时间有限，建议优先读：Introduction；主定理与对应证明思路；entropy geometry 那一节；最后的 stabilization 部分。图表上重点看展示 sensitivity 随 ηT 增长的数值图，以及 anchored 变体与原始 MD 的对比图。预计 45 到 60 分钟可以抓住主线；若要真正吃透，证明里 dual-primal 映射如何制造乘法放大值得细看。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies finite-time sensitivity of mirror descent to initialization. The question is simple but important: when optimization starts from a pretrained, SFT, or already aligned checkpoint rather than a random point, can a tiny perturbation in the starting model be amplified by the update dynamics? The answer is yes, and the amplification can be exponentially worse than gradient descent. The authors show that for non-quadratic regularizers, even with convex smooth objectives and even when the regularizer is strongly convex, smooth, and well-conditioned in Euclidean norm, the output after T steps can differ by e^{Ω(ηT)} under an ε-sized initialization perturbation. This sharply contrasts with the stability of GD and quadratic mirror maps. For KL-regularized policy optimization and LLM post-training, where the initialization and the reference model are part of the optimization setup, this directly affects reproducibility and checkpoint sensitivity.","importance":"$5a","technical_approach":"$5b","experiments":"$5c","reading_guide":"$5d","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇会修正一个常见偷懒 prior：KL/镜像几何不自动带来稳定后训练；读定理和构造就够，别把它误读成工程结论。","en":"This corrects a lazy prior: KL/mirror geometry does not automatically make post-training stable; read the theorem and construction, not as an engineering claim."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Shira Vansover-Hager","Matan Schliserman","Ofir Schlisselberg","Tomer Koren"],"notable_authors":["Tomer Koren"],"tags":["mirror-descent","optimization-stability","initialization-sensitivity","RLHF-theory","convex-analysis"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11431","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.11431","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.11660","title":"Bergson: An Open Source Library for Data Attribution","abstract":"Data attribution is a promising field in interpretability that aims to explain model behavior through the influence of its training data, with applications including debugging undesirable model behavior and training dataset curation. th","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-10T04:56:45Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_11660.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的不是新的 introduce a new attribution theory. It addresses a persistent systems gap: many data attribution methods work only in small-model, single-node, offline settings, but become hard to reproduce or compare once you move to large language models, large training corpora, and distributed environments. The goal is to turn influence functions, approximate unrolled differentiation, and exact differentiation through training into a unified, scalable, and reproducible toolkit. We present a Bayesian theory of feature learning in attention; we then focus on how the copy subcircuit in the first layer of an induction head is learned by analyzing a single-layer softmax attention network trained on a copy task. We derive a closed-form posterior over the attention matrix and reduce it to a low-dimensional ord","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-10T13:26:56Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_12058.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["stat.ML","cond-mat.dis-nn","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解释一个很具体、但对理解 ICL 很关键的问题：为什么 transformer 里与 induction head 相关的 copy 子电路会在训练中突然出现，而不是平滑形成。作者不直接从大模型整体入手，而是把问题压缩到一个可解析的最小设置：单层、单头 softmax attention 网络，在 copy task 上学习从上下文中把匹配 token 拷贝出来。核心目标不是再做一次经验观察，而是给出一个能从训练数据量出发、预测 attention 模式何时从“无效/均匀聚合”跳到“定点拷贝”的理论机制。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有两层。第一，它给“能力突现”提供了一个比经验曲线更扎实的解释框架：至少在 copy/induction 这一类 ICL 基元上，突变可以来自 attention 机制自身的相变结构，而不只是评测指标离散化造成的假象。第二，它把 softmax attention 和 linear attention 的学习动力学差异讲清楚了：softmax 对应一阶相变，linear attention 更像连续的二阶转变再接一个 crossover。这对架构选择有直接启发，因为它说明不同 attention 激活不只是算力/效率取舍，还会改变能力形成的形态、训练信号的锐利程度，以及 loss 曲线里能否看到明显阶段切换。","technical_approach":"方法上的关键是一个 Bayesian feature learning 分析。作者先对单头 attention 网络做权重边缘化，得到 attention matrix 的闭式后验分布；然后再把这个高维对象约化到两个低维 order parameters，上面分别刻画“均匀注意”与“copy 注意”的竞争强度。这样一来，训练过程不再被看成参数空间里难以解释的优化轨迹，而是落在一个可分析的自由能/后验景观上。论文的核心发现是：随着训练数据量增加，后验主导态会发生结构切换。对 softmax attention，这个切换是离散跳变，存在明显的双稳态竞争和一阶相变特征；对 linear attention，则先连续增强 copy 相关方向，再经历更平滑的 crossover。作者还把这些 order parameters 映射回可观测的 loss signature，解释了为什么训练中会先学到 in-context unigram 统计，再学到 bigram 级别的定点复制。和以往 lazy training、NNGP 或冻结 attention pattern 的分析不同，这里真正把 attention pattern 本身作为主要自由度来处理，而且显式捕捉到非高斯激活与层间耦合的作用。","experiments":"实验部分的作用主要是验证理论，而不是追求大规模性能。作者用 Bayesian sampling 和标准 Adam 训练两条线去对照理论预测，发现 attention 模式随数据量变化的转折位置、loss 形状以及阶段性学习顺序都与理论一致。关键现象包括：softmax attention 在某个数据规模附近会从近似均匀聚合突然切到 copy 模式，loss 上对应明显的尖锐变化；linear attention 则没有同样锋利的跳变，而是更连续地过渡。论文还把这一结果与已有两层 transformer 在 Markov 序列上的三阶段学习现象对齐：先不利用上下文，再利用上下文 unigram，最后利用 bigram。这里的理论最有价值的实验结论不是某个百分点提升，而是“相变类型—attention 机制—loss 签名—ICL 子能力形成”之间形成了闭环证据。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先看引言里对 induction head、copy subcircuit 和三阶段学习现象的定位，再看给出 order parameter 的理论主结果和 softmax/linear attention 对比图。真正值得停下来读的是：一，作者如何从权重积分推到 attention matrix 的后验；二，为什么两个标量 order parameters 就足以描述主导行为；三，softmax 为何会产生一阶相变，而 linear attention 只能给出连续转变。读图时重点看 attention pattern 随数据规模变化的相图，以及 loss signature 与 unigram→bigram 学习阶段的对应关系。预计 30-45 分钟可以抓住主线；如果关心 mechanistic interpretability 与训练动力学的连接，再回头细读推导部分。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a narrow but important question for in-context learning: why does the copy subcircuit associated with induction heads appear abruptly during training instead of forming smoothly? Rather than analyzing full-scale LLMs, it studies a tractable setting: a single-layer, single-head attention network trained on a copy task. The goal is not just to reproduce the empirical observation of abrupt emergence, but to derive a mechanism that predicts when attention shifts from ineffective or uniform pooling to position-specific copying as data increases.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is conceptual rather than benchmark-driven. First, it gives a concrete account of capability emergence: at least for the copy primitive underlying induction behavior, abrupt changes can arise from the internal phase structure of attention itself, not only from thresholded evaluation metrics. Second, it clarifies that softmax and linear attention do not merely differ in efficiency or approximation quality; they induce different learning transitions. Softmax yields a first-order transition, while linear attention shows a continuous second-order transition followed by a crossover. That matters for architecture design because the attention nonlinearity changes how sharply capabilities form, how training signals appear in loss curves, and how easy it is to detect stage changes during training.","technical_approach":"$63","experiments":"The experiments are designed to validate the theory rather than to maximize downstream performance. Using both Bayesian sampling and standard Adam training, the authors show that the predicted transition points, loss signatures, and stagewise learning behavior match empirical training dynamics. The key observation is that softmax attention undergoes a sharp switch from near-uniform pooling to copy behavior around a critical data scale, with a corresponding abrupt change in loss. Linear attention does not show the same sharp jump and instead transitions more gradually. The paper also aligns these findings with prior observations in two-layer transformers trained on Markov sequences: first no use of context, then in-context unigram statistics, and finally bigram statistics. The most useful experimental takeaway is not a headline metric, but the closed loop between transition type, attention mechanism, loss signature, and the emergence of an ICL-relevant subcircuit.","reading_guide":"For a quick pass, start with the introduction sections that connect induction heads, the copy subcircuit, and the known three-stage learning dynamics. Then read the main theoretical result introducing the order parameters and the comparison between softmax and linear attention. The parts worth slower reading are: how the posterior over the attention matrix is derived after integrating out weights; why two scalar order parameters are enough to capture the dominant behavior; and why softmax produces a first-order transition while linear attention does not. Focus on the figures showing phase behavior as a function of data scale and the mapping from order parameters to loss signatures. Around 30-45 minutes is enough to get the main idea; the derivations are worth deeper study if you care about mechanistic interpretability and training dynamics.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"对 induction head 形成机制的硬核理论分析，揭示了 softmax 和 linear attention 在相变类型上的本质差异，做 mechanistic interpretability 的必看。","en":"Hardcore theoretical analysis of induction head formation, revealing fundamental differences in phase transition types between softmax and linear attention. A must-read for mechanistic interpretability researchers."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Itay Lavie","Kirsten Fischer","Andrey Lekov","Frederic Van Maele","Zohar Ringel","Moritz Helias"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Phase Transition","Attention Mechanism","Induction Heads","Bayesian Theory","Mechanistic Interpretability"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12058","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.12058","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.11198","title":"The Structural Attention Tax: How Retrieval Format Hijacks In-Context Learning Independent of Content","abstract":"Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems inject external knowledge to improve LLM outputs, yet the format of injected content -- distinct from its semantic relevance -- can independently distort the model's attention distribution. We identify and formalise a phenomenon we term the structural attention tax: knowledge graph (KG) triples, due to their relational delimiters and repeated slot patterns, capture 2-3x more attention per token than semantically equivalent natural-language text ($\\hat","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-21T08:47:00Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_11198.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的不是“检索到什么”，而是“检索结果以什么格式进入上下文”。作者要解决的核心问题是：在 RAG 或 augmented ICL 中，结构化知识片段，尤其是 KG triples，会不会仅仅因为分隔符、槽位重复、关系词模板这些表面结构，就系统性地吸走注意力，从而挤压 demonstrations 和任务相关上下文的注意力预算。论文把这个现象定义为 structural attention tax，并试图把“格式偏置”和“语义价值”拆开分析：前者决定注意力被转移多少，后者决定这种转移究竟帮忙还是添乱。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要有两层。第一，它提醒人们 transformer 的上下文竞争不只受语义驱动，还受 token 级结构先验影响；这对 prompt 设计、RAG 接口、训练时数据格式混合都有直接意义。第二，它把 retrieval/source quality 与 prompt formatting 分成两条正交轴：语义轴决定检索内容是否有用，结构轴决定这些内容会不会不成比例地占据模型计算资源。这个区分对做长上下文预训练、合成数据模板设计、跨模态离散 token 接口的人都很有参考价值。不过量级上，这更像一个机制分析与接口设计提醒，而不是会直接改写基座训练 recipe 的工作。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者建立了一个 attention decomposition 框架。给定 prompt x=[I;D;K;q]，其中 I 是 instruction，D 是 demonstrations，K 是外部知识，q 是 query。作者把 query token 对知识区 K 的注意力分配写成语义项和结构项的组合，其中结构项由 λ·σ(K) 表示。σ(K) 是 structural intensity，来自三类可观测模式：结构分隔符密度、关系/槽位 token 的出现频率、以及局部重复模式。λ 则被视为模型内生的结构敏感系数，依赖层和头。这个分解的关键不是精确恢复真实注意力生成机制，而是提供一个可检验的工作假设：即使语义内容相同，只要格式更“结构化”，也会获得更高 attention mass。\n\n实验设计围绕“格式-内容解耦”展开。作者构造七种条件，把相同语义内容分别写成自然语言、相关 triples、无关 triples、噪声 triples 等形式，比较它们对 demonstrations 区域 attention 的压缩效应。论文还提出一个 compression bound，用来说明在总 attention 归一化约束下，K 区域结构强度升高会怎样压缩 D 区域的可用注意力。\n\n缓解方法部分给了五类策略，核心思路都是削弱 λ·σ(K)。其中真正做了实验的主要有两类。S3 format flattening：把 triples 改写成更接近自然语言或弱结构化文本，降低 delimiter 和 slot pattern 的显著性。S1 structural dispersal：把高度规则的结构打散，减少局部重复和模板集中度。另有 logit suppression、训练期正则等方案主要停留在理论或算法草图层面。","experiments":"实验覆盖两个开源模型家族：Mistral-7B 和 LLaMA-3-8B；任务是三类 QA。最核心的结果有四点。\n\n第一，KG triples 的 attention capture 明显高于中性自然语言。文中给出的代表性数字是，KG 区域的归一化结构强度估计约为 0.70，而 neutral text 约为 0.25；对应地，triples 每 token 吸收的注意力大约是自然语言的 2 到 3 倍。\n\n第二，这种 attention capture 与语义是否相关关系不大。相关 triples、无关 triples、甚至噪声 triples 的 attention pattern 接近，说明模型先被格式吸引，再谈内容是否有用。这是全文最关键的证据，因为它支撑了“结构税独立于语义价值”的主张。\n\n第三，demonstration attention 会被明显压缩，文中报告最高可达 42%。这说明问题不只是局部 token 被多看几眼，而是 prompt 内部的竞争格局被改写，进而影响 ICL 行为。\n\n第四，在 source selection 上，任务匹配的 BM25 retrieval 相比不匹配的 ConceptNet retrieval，在 HotpotQA 上有超过 30 个百分点的优势，而更复杂的 gating 带来的收益不超过 2 个百分点。作者据此强调，先选对知识源，再谈精细 gating；语义对齐的重要性远大于在错误知识源上做复杂控制。\n\n缓解实验里，S3 format flattening 得到较稳定支持：既改善准确率，也在 attention-level 分析中降低了结构区的吸附效应。S1 structural dispersal 结果较混合，存在模型依赖，说明“打散结构”未必总能转化为更好的任务表现，可能因为它同时损伤了可解析性或局部可读性。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先抓三处。\n\n第一，看 Introduction 和 Figure 1。这里把问题讲得最清楚：attention budget 是固定的，结构化片段会竞争性地挤压 demonstrations。只要接受这个设定，后面的实验就容易理解。\n\n第二，看 Section 3 的分解公式和 Proposition 1。这里不必过度纠结形式化是否严格到可作为理论定理使用，重点是理解作者如何把“结构强度”定义成可测量量，以及为什么它能导出 compression 预测。\n\n第三，看 Section 5 concentra","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-10T12:26:49Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_12003.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个推理时选择问题：经典 self-consistency 依赖最终答案的精确匹配，因此适合数学选择题、短答案问答这类闭式输出；一旦任务变成代码生成、摘要、开放式文本生成，语义等价的答案在表面形式上往往差异很大，majority voting 就失效了。作者想回答的不是单纯“怎样做一个更好的 reranker”，而是更基础的问题：开放式生成里的“一致性”到底该如何定义，是否可以用表示空间中的几何结构来刻画。","importance":"对预训练本身不是直接方法贡献，但对基座模型评估和推理时采样机制有启发。价值主要有三点。第一，它把 self-consistency 从符号匹配扩展到连续表示空间，给开放式任务提供了一个训练外、模型外的统一视角。第二，它提示我们：生成质量与表示空间中的密度、中心性、聚类结构有关，这对理解采样分布、hidden state 可分性、以及“为什么多样采样后再选优有效”有分析价值。第三，它给出一个比 LLM-as-a-judge 更轻、更稳定的选择信号，适合做推理时增强，但贡献量级仍偏方法性和分析性，不属于会改写预训练范式的工作。","technical_approach":"方法很直接。给定同一个输入，先从模型独立采样 n 个候选输出；再用一个嵌入函数把每个输出映射到表示空间；然后在这个空间里估计“agreement”。作者提出的 EBA（Embedding-Based Agreement）不是训练一个新打分器，而是利用候选输出在表示空间中的聚集程度来做代理一致性信号。核心假设是：语义兼容、质量较高的输出会形成更致密的区域，而错误、离群、低质量输出更分散。\n\n从全文描述看，EBA 的关键步骤包括：1）对 sampled generations 取句向量或序列级表示；2）在嵌入空间中做聚类或近邻密度估计，识别主导语义簇；3）从最大簇或最中心区域中选代表输出。作者还系统比较了不同表示来源：外部 embedding model、模型自身 hidden representation、不同模型家族的表示空间。一个重要点是，他们不是把 embedding 仅当作语义相似度工具，而是把“簇大小 + 中心性”解释为 self-consistency 的连续版本。\n\n与已有方法相比，区别主要在三处。相对 exact-match majority voting，它不要求 canonical answer form；相对 LLM-based evaluator，它不需要额外 judge 模型，也避免 judge 偏好和额外推理成本；相对 uncertainty-based selection，它不是看单条样本的 token-level 不确定性，而是看多样本集合的几何组织。作者还强调，闭式任务中的 majority vote 可以看成是这种几何观点的离散极限：等价答案在表示空间里塌缩到同一区域。","experiments":"实验覆盖三类任务：MATH500、HumanEval、CNN/DM，总体目的是验证表示空间 agreement 是否能跨 closed-form 与 open-ended 任务稳定工作。摘要和正文给出的主结论比较一致：EBA 相比随机选样本和若干已有选择策略都有稳定提升，并且在模型家族、模型规模、embedding 空间选择上都较稳。\n\n更值得看的不是单个绝对分数，而是几组分析结论。第一，随着采样数增加，agreement 信号更强，说明这种方法依赖的是“样本集合的组织结构”，不是单条输出的局部特征。第二，EBA 的 scaling 行为比 LLM-evaluator 和 uncertainty-based 方法更平滑，意味着当模型变大、采样变多时，它的选择信号没有明显失稳。第三，作者发现表示空间中的中心区域与高质量输出显著相关，边缘区域的样本更容易出错；这点对理解生成分布很有意思。第四，在数学这类闭式任务上，EBA 与经典 self-consistency 高度一致，支持其“统一解释”主张。\n\n从你给的全文片段里，没有看到完整表格数字、采样数配置、具体提升幅度和各 embedding backbone 的详细排名，因此不能编造精确数值。阅读原文时应重点补齐：每个任务的 n 值、聚类算法、表示抽取层、与 baselines 的绝对提升、以及 HumanEval/CNN-DM 上的代价对比。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，重点放在三部分。先看引言和方法开头，确认作者真正想解决的是“agreement 的定义”而不是普通 reranking。再看实验里的三组分析：采样数变化、不同 embedding space 的稳健性、中心性与质量的关系。这三处比主结果表更有信息量。最后看作者如何把 closed-form majority voting 解释成几何
